# RENTipid GLCC v1.1 — Global Wave 1 Class C Multilingual Review Dossier

**Controlling Master:** RENTIPID-GLCC-MULTILINGUAL-MIP-001 v1.0  
**Governance Scope:** GLOBAL-W1-E Consolidated Multilingual Legal Review  
**Total Class C Controlled Keys:** 241  
**Total Target Languages Covered:** 32  
**Overall Legal Approval Status:** `PENDING` (No Fabricated Reviewers / Strict Governance Boundary)  
**Jurisdiction Independence:** Fully Preserved — UI language does NOT alter statutory baseline or applicable jurisdiction.  

---

## Executive Summary

This dossier consolidates all 241 Class C Controlled Legal & Compliance message keys across all 32 supported target language packages (including preserved `en-US` and 31 newly adapted global languages).

Each controlled key includes:
1. Canonical Key and Authoritative Source ID
2. Canonical English Source Text and SHA-256 Checksum
3. Complete target translations across all languages with cryptographic checksums
4. Jurisdiction Independence Statement
5. Legal approval status: `PENDING` awaiting batch review by qualified compliance authorities.
